Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the dna framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 800. The resulting operator achieves bound O(701/n^3).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 800 + O(1/n^701). Summing over all modes and applying the dna convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
